# Repo Pulse benchmark report

Generated by `packages/security/src/benchmark/cli.ts` (W18-17). Deterministic layers only: the in-process fast lane (anti_slop arithmetic and graph checks, secrets rulepack) and the YAML rulepacks. No LLM tier is measured.

## Public benchmarks, by CWE

Measured 2026-10-16 at 4eb5c06a7666 with opengrep 1.27.1.

TPR is the share of real vulnerabilities found; FPR is the share of safe look-alikes flagged; Youden is TPR − FPR (0 = a coin flip, 1 = perfect, negative = worse than guessing). A CWE we have no rule for shows a TPR of 0%, and stays in the table.

## Tool adapters: precision and the promotion gate

Each row is one tool rule, scored raw (before the cross-tool dedupe that decides which of two tools' reports of one defect is stored), on our own labelled fixtures. Promotion out of shadow is an admin action in the rule registry on at least 20 findings, 30 days and under 20% false positives from the workspace's own triage. A benchmark can supply the first and last in kind and never the days or the triage, so **no rule below is promoted**; the last column is how far the benchmark half of the evidence gets. Sample sizes are small (a rule has the one to three cases written for it), so a precision of 100% here means "no false positive in those cases", not "precise".
